2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月 ISM 非製造業 PMI 從 8 月的 55.4 降至 54.9，顯示服務業活動增速放
緩，然而國內需求強勁使供應鏈承壓，並推動投入價格指數由 72.6 升至 74.0 為
四年多來新高；另一方面，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心指數由 9 月的 48.1
降至 46.3，為連續第三個月下降且創 5 月以來最低 ， 惟未來一年通膨預期由 4.6%
升至 4.7%，顯示通膨可能持續維持高位至 2027 年。
⚫ 聯準會 9 月 FOMC 會議紀要顯示與會官員均支持升息，並認為年底前尚需升息
一次 ，但對於後續升息時點及升息理由上存在分歧； 其中部分官員認為升息是防
止能源及其他價格衝擊造成更廣泛影響 的預防性措施，但更為鷹派官員則認為
升息在於抑制投資和支出，以防範正在顯現的需求驅動型通膨。
⚫ 歐洲央行 9 月貨幣政策會議紀要顯示全體決策委員均同意升息，並認為當前經
濟成長風險偏向下行 ， 通膨風險則偏向上行 ，因此將保留政策彈性， 根據經濟數

據做為決策，不提供未來利率路徑指引。
⚫ 台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨警
戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ，除了豪雨導致蔬果價格上漲及中秋假期的短期因
素外，油價居高不下亦使通膨壓力增加。
⚫ 中國央行發布匯率政策立場聲明， 強調中國貿易發展根源於產業國際競爭力的
提升，無必要通過匯率貶值獲取貿易競爭優勢 ，並指出中國長期實施以市場供需
為基礎、參考一籃子貨幣進行調節管理浮動匯率制度。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.699，低點在 31.940，以 31.875 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 4.5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，受到前週公布的美國非農就業數據低於預期，降低
市場對聯準會 10 月升息的預期， 台股創高吸引外資匯入 ， 推升台幣最高來到31.699；
不過隨著美伊緊張情勢升溫且台灣連假將至 ， 台股收低使外資轉為大幅賣超並匯出 ，
推低台幣回到 9 月中以來的震盪區間下緣，最低觸及 31.940，最終在央行調節之下
收在 31.875。 
＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 10 月密西根大學消費者信心
指數持續下滑，由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創 5 月以來最低，惟未來一年通膨預期
升至 4.7%，加上中東局勢反覆，油價高位震盪，仍支撐市場對於聯準會年內升息
的預期，預計美元指數維持在 102 附近震盪，對台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台灣 9 月出口創歷史新高，年增 60.9%，反映 AI 及科技需求持續強勁，出口商後
續拋匯需求可望提供台幣支撐；然而，台股逼近五萬點關卡之際，外資未能延續買
超走勢，且連假前已大幅賣超台股，若後續外資持續匯出，將限制台幣升值空間。
綜合以上，預估本週台幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.700-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/12/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣區間震盪 。 中國十一長假期間 (10/1~10/7) 境內人民幣市場休市 ， 而
境外人民幣維持偏強格局 ， 主要在6.7000 上方震盪 ， 臨近周末 ， 人行中間價為6.7330，
創 3 年多來新高，加上美元指數漲勢放緩，推升境外人民幣突破 6.7000 整數關卡，
終場境外人民幣收在 6.6927，週線升值 134 點，境內人民幣收在 6.6929，較十一長
假前夕升值 121 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間偏升。就國際市場來看，美國密西根大學消費者信心指數
由 9 月的 48.1 降至 46.3，創5 月以來新低 ， 不過對於未來一年的通膨預期升至4.7%，
加上油價維持高檔 ， 市場對於聯準會年內升息的預期仍存 ， 美元指數維持高檔震盪 ，
將限制人民幣升值速度；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價維持偏強設定，不過人
行表示將防範短期大幅波動與急貶風險 ， 並無意透過人民幣貶值取得貿易競爭優勢 ，
有助於穩定市場對人民幣的預期 ；最後就技術面來看 ，USD/CNH 日線圖中 ， 各天期
均線仍向下發散，空頭格局不變，下方支撐先看向 9 月底低點 6.6908。綜合以上，
預估本週人民幣區間偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6800-6.7100

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪走升。週初，因美元指數在102 附近盤整，帶領USD/JPY 延
續前一週走勢震盪走升到 158 上方；週間，因缺乏重要經濟數據，市場偏向觀望，
USD/JPY 維持在 158 附近震盪；臨近週末，USD/JPY 曾一度回測 157.50 附近，不
過在美日利差的主導下，USD/JPY 依舊保持偏多的走勢，終場收在 158.31，週線上
漲 84 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、路透短觀調查顯示，受半導
體強勁訂單推動，日本 10 月大型製造業信心升至正 22，創近五年新高；但原料成
本高漲重擊非製造業，信心指數降至正 23。與此同時，此前投下升息反對票的日銀
鴿派委員佐藤綾野立場軟化，表態支持分階段升息，市場對後續正常化預期升溫，
預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間 。二 、 日本首相高市早苗表示密切關注匯率與物價
走勢 ， 強調日本已脫離通縮 ， 將完全尊重央行政策獨立性 ， 暗示不反對進一步升息 。
面對通膨加劇與日圓走貶壓力，其過往堅持的貨幣寬鬆立場已出現轉向。然而目前
美日實質利差仍大，持續為 USD/JPY 提供支撐。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，
USD/JPY 持續沿著上彎的月線上攻，然匯價持續承壓於季線下方，在有效站上季線
及突破前高壓力前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震
盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：157.10-159.50。 
前週交易區間 
157.41 158.50

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Oct/12/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 震盪 走跌 。週初， 因法國債券風險與區域政治不確定性 ，
EUR/USD 下行最低來到 1.1160； 週間 ，受中東地緣風險升溫與歐洲財政疑慮帶動避
險需求 ， 加上美債殖利率維持高檔 ， 美元利差優勢延續，歐元維持低檔； 臨近週末 ，
逢低歐元有買盤支撐，帶動歐元收復部分跌幅，終場收在 1.1201，週線下跌 46 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學
家連恩表示，夏末能源成本飆升為經濟增長帶來下行風險，並可能限制央行收緊貨
幣政策的力道，與此同時，歐元區 10 月 Sentix 投資者信心指數由 5.1 降至 2.7，德
國預期指數同步滑落，反映市場對經濟復甦的信心明顯降溫，預計將令歐元持續承
壓。二、法國社會動盪與支出訴求阻礙財政減赤進程，加劇債務可持續性疑慮，並
推升法德公債利差與信用風險，令歐元承壓走弱。相較美國經濟展現韌性且聯準會
維持偏鷹立場，歐洲深陷景氣放緩與財政掣肘，歐洲央行緊縮空間受限。在歐美基
本面與政策前景持續分歧下 ，EUR/USD 缺乏實質支撐 ， 後市將維持偏弱整理並下探
關鍵支撐。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖多次向下測試 1.1165 關
鍵價位均獲得支撐，惟每次反彈均無法站上五日線，且各天期均線下彎呈現空頭排
列。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。

＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1100-1.1260 
前週交易區間 
1.1160 1.1276

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.10.05-115.10.08 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.66% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.05% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.82%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分 ， 本週邁入新的提存期 ， 央行連日減發存單 ， 令市場資金供
給充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週一央行標售 364 天期定存單 1100 億元 ， 加權平均得標利率為1.602%， 低於
市場預期區間 1.61%-1.635%，較上期標售結果下跌 4.1 個基本點。 
週 四 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 受美債利率續創新高影響，週四 10 年期指標券 A15109 成交在 2.05%，創同
年期券自 2008 年以來高點，5 年期指標券 A15108 則未成交，最近成交利率
為 1.82%。 
台灣 9 月 CPI 年增率自 8 月 2.05%上升至 2.73%，已連續 5 個月超過 2%通膨
警戒線 ， 且創兩年半來最大漲幅 ， 目前油價仍居高難下 ， 通膨壓力將令央行維
持偏緊的貨幣政策，加上國際債市利率持續走升，台債殖利率恐面臨上行壓
力，預估 5 年券區間為 1.85%~1.95%，10 年券區間為 2.0%~2.10%。

## Document 093: 2026年9月投資月報

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月投資月報.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資月報)
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
科技動能支撐股市，利率風險牽動債市配置
2026 年 9 月
全球股市 8 月截至 26 日多數上漲，其中成熟市場上漲 3.1%居冠，新興亞洲、東協股市與標普
500 也分別上漲 2.9%、2.7%及 2.6%。美股受企業財報韌性與月初就業、通膨數據降溫支撐；亞洲
則受美元偏弱、油價與長端殖利率回落帶動風險偏好改善。然而，中國股市期間下跌 0.3%，該國 7
月零售、工業生產與投資數據偏弱，加上阿里巴巴折價配股一度衝擊科技股，惟公司高層後續增持有
助緩解信心壓力 。 拉美股市下跌0.8%，在主要股市中敬陪末座 ， 主要拖累來自巴西 ；8 月截至 24 日，

外資淨流出巴西股市約 214 億雷亞爾，加上財政與 10 月大選的不確定性壓抑市場。印度亦小跌
0.3%，因高油價與 RBI 偏鷹訊號使公債殖利率上升。
全球債市 8 月截至 26 日則全面收紅 ， 以新興當地債上漲1.53%居冠 ， 美元指數回落至99 附近，
為其帶來匯兌加成；亞洲高收益債與全球高收益債分別上漲 1.16%、1.09%，高票息與較短存續期提
供支撐 。 亞洲債券 、 全球主權債與新興美元債表現相對落後 。8 月中美國 30 年期殖利率一度升至 2007
年以來高點，壓抑長存續期債券；其後油價回落，加上8/19 財政部宣布 9/9 起將 10 至 30 年期公債
買回上限至少倍增至每次 40 億美元，長端流動性疑慮獲緩解，30 年期殖利率回落，因而帶動債券價
格修復。
展望 9 月，市場焦點將集中於 9 月 15 日至 16 日的 FOMC 會議，以及最新經濟預測與點陣圖；
全球股市預料呈現高檔震盪格局。儘管物價漲勢稍有緩和，但 Fed 對通膨仍保持警戒；若政策立場
較市場預期鷹派，可能推升美債殖利率波動，並壓抑高估值成長股走勢。不過，AI 基礎建設與企業

科技投資趨勢延續，美國企業獲利成長亦有利科技股表現，預料中期基本面仍具支撐。台灣受 AI 與
半導體出口需求帶動，經濟成長動能強勁；日股則持續受企業治理改革與資本效率改善支撐。整體而
言，9 月股市短線震盪難免，但中期仍看好科技股，以及基本面相對強勁的台灣與日本股市。
就債市而言，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔；若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利
率上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短的存續期間亦伴隨再投資
風險；10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。相較之下，3 至 10 年期債券兼具較高息收、
適度存續期間與參與利率下行的空間。整體而言，目前 3 至 10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬
條件相對均衡，較具表現機會。

美國股市：AI 熱潮推升企業獲利，美股續強但留意利率風險
八月美股在高檔震盪中收高，科技股表現亮眼，除整體美股優異財報支撐指數表現外，AI 龍頭企
業如 NVIDIA 均給出強勁展望 ，AI 行情由晶片硬體向軟體與資安應用端擴散 。指數上揚主要由輝達、
微軟、亞馬遜、帕蘭提爾、特斯拉、Meta、美光科技、賽富時、Marvell、默克藥廠等企業帶動，表
現相對弱勢的族群包括蘋果、博通、沃爾瑪、TJX、AppLovin、威騰、聯合健康、CVS、應用材料、
超微半導體等。
聯準會新任主席試圖以新的溝通模式取代傳統前瞻指引，市場解讀為基準利率有上行空間且更長

時間維持高檔。聯準會主席華許在全球央行年會(Jackson Hole)拒絕提供傳統的前瞻指引或機械式的
反應函數，強調央行過度承諾未來利率路徑會失去政策彈性，以一套「決策準則」取代指引，重點觀
察潛在通膨是否快速回到 2%、勞動市場是否偏離充分就業，以及金融條件是否有效限制總需求。由
於 7 月 FOMC 會議出現官員主張升息的情況，使短端與長端美債殖利率走勢分化，殖利率曲線進一
步陡峭化 ， 市場正在質疑 Fed 是否具備一致且可理解的決策框架 ， 但在華許發言後 ， 按照這套標準，
目前政策訊號仍偏向利率在較高水準維持更長時間。由於通膨問題仍具廣度，同時信用利差偏低、股
市高檔、AI 投資與私人需求持續強勁，很難將目前金融條件視為具有限制性，故市場預期 聯準會可
能不只提高升息機率，也提高了升息後維持高利率的可能性，甚至進一步提高未來降息的門檻。
第二季財報公布大致落幕，表現優於先前預期。目前標普 500 成分股已有 459 家公布 2Q26 財
報，其中 86%獲利優於市場預期，並帶動 2Q26 獲利年增達約 31.6%，較季初市場預估的 21%大幅

上修；全年獲利年增亦由 23%上修至 27%。類股獲利年增動能以能源 (YoY+145%)、資訊科技
(YoY+71%)、原材料(YoY+41%)增長動能最強，醫療保健類股(YoY-7%)表現落後。資訊科技類股獲
利成長主要由半導體族群帶動，次產業中半導體獲利年增 139%、科技硬體與設備獲利年增 50.9%。
美國期中選舉前的「資料中心用電排擠效應引發的民粹反彈」持續發酵。隨著 AI 資料中心龐大
的電力需求，引發地方民意對民生與傳統產業用電資源遭擠壓的疑慮持續升溫 。 各州政府在選舉壓力
下，部分地方政府緊急凍結資料中心計畫，預料 11 月期中選舉後政治干擾將可降溫。
目前標普 500 指數位於歷史高檔位置，本益比已升至 23.51 倍、股價淨值比達 5.6 倍，短期評價
偏高。雖然美國科技業者目前仍居 AI 產業領先地位，但股市漲幅已高，短期仍需留意市場過熱與估
值偏高風險，建議以長線角度分批布局。

歐洲股市：景氣溫和復甦，歐洲央行升息預期與政治變數限制評價 
八月歐股延續多頭走勢，泛歐600 指數中原物料與科技股表現相對強勢，包括思愛普、艾司摩爾
控股、Argenx、施耐德電機、力拓、嘉能可、英美資源、安聯集團、勞斯萊斯控股、西門子等表現
較佳；相較之下，部分金融與消費股表現偏弱，包括雀巢、匯豐控股、諾和諾德、英美菸草、阿斯特
捷利康、法國巴黎銀行、法國興業銀行、諾華、百威英博、萬喜等。 
歐元區八月綜合 PMI 升至 52.1，連續第 2 個月站上榮枯線，顯示第 3 季初景氣延續擴張。其中
製造業升至 52.8，創 2022 年 5 月以來最佳表現，復甦已由生產端擴散至需求端。AI 相關科技與設
備需求增加，加上國防支出擴大，帶動製造業需求回升；服務業則持平於 51.7，受惠於旅遊維持溫
和擴張。國家別來看，德國製造業 PMI 升至 54.1，創 2022 年以來最佳表現，法國製造業重返擴張
區間，但極端高溫拖累服務業活動。整體而言，八月 PMI 顯示歐元區景氣動能明顯改善，製造業加
速復甦，短期衰退風險進一步下降，然而服務業擴張力道未見加快，復甦步調呈現分化。 
部分歐洲央行官員表示，九月已有充分理由升息，利率交換市場目前反映 ECB 在 9 月會議升息
一碼的可能性。美伊戰爭推高能源成本，加上歐洲經濟表現穩健，使市場認為 ECB 仍有收緊貨幣政
策的空間。市場焦點因此由「ECB 會不會升息」轉向「升息循環還會延續多久」 。中東局勢仍是最大
變數，若能源成本持續走高且逐步傳導至商品、服務價格與薪資，ECB 第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場。 
9 月歐股須留意政治局勢。若 AfD（德國另類選擇黨）於地方選舉取得大勝，雖不會直接改變目
前政府執政地位，但可能削弱改革推進速度，造成市場風險溢價上升；另外英國新任首相柏南承諾維
持既有財政規則，並受到工黨不提高所得稅、國民保險及主要稅率承諾的約束，使政府在國防、公共
服務與生活成本支出增加之際 ， 財政操作空間相當有限 ， 市場因此高度關注政府是否轉向其他形式的
增稅。若改革措施過度激進推高英國公債殖利率，恐將造成市場震盪。 
歐洲經濟整體呈現溫和復甦，但原油價格再次走高是最大隱憂。若能源成本居高不下，歐洲央行
與英國央行第四季仍可能維持鷹派立場 ， 進而導致產業表現分化 。 銀行與金融類股可望受惠於利率走
高及淨利差改善；反之，科技、醫療保健及其他成長型類股將面臨殖利率上升所帶來的評價壓力。目
前泛歐 600 指數本益比約 16.5 倍，股價淨值比約 2.5 倍。雖然俄烏戰爭與通膨再度升溫風險仍需關
注，但在評價合理的背景下，歐股仍適合作為長線分批布局標的。

資料來源：彭博,2026/8/31

日本股市：通膨回溫拉高升息預期，半導體與內需動能推升經濟表現
八月日股受地緣政治風險與殖利率走高影響，日經225 指數劇烈震盪。盤面上，強勢族群包括瑞
可利控股、愛德萬測試、藤倉、軟銀、松下控股、村田製作所、瑞穗金融、東京威力科創、三菱UFJ
金融、揖斐電等；弱勢股包括東京海上控股、迅銷、大金工業、富士軟片、日本電裝、發那科、7&I、
丸紅、損保控股、KDDI 等。
8 月東京核心 CPI 升至 1.8%，雖能源補貼與食品價格降溫壓抑整體物價，但租金與醫療服務價
格明顯上升；扣除食品與能源後通膨加速至 1.4%，顯示內生性通膨壓力仍在。若就十大產品分項來

看，引導本月通膨的上漲主要來自房屋及醫療保健，其中租金價格年增幅達 1.9%，反映物價上漲持
續傳遞至房市 ； 醫療保健價格年增幅亦自前月的1.4%攀升至 2.8%， 因政府將高額療養費制度內的自
付費用上限調高所致 。 根據總務省估算 ， 日本政府對能源補貼的政策約壓抑本月通膨0.27 個百分點，
顯示若扣除政策影響，則當前通膨約為 2.2%，仍高於日本央行所設定的物價目標。雖政府補貼政策
將持續對通膨帶來壓抑效果，使得整體通膨略低於 2%，然而搭配央行對日圓匯率過於弱勢、恐進一
步墊高通膨的擔憂，預估將促使日本央行最快於 9 月份升息一碼。
日本半導體製造裝置協會 （SEAJ） 公布7 月日本製晶片設備銷售額 （3 個月移動平均值） 為5,562.2
億日圓，月銷售額年增 35.4%，創下 2024 年 12 月以來最大增幅。同時，若觀察今年 1 至 7 月份累
計銷售額達 3.44 兆日圓，年增 15.7%，顯示隨著當前人工智慧墊高晶片需求，推升廠商對半導體設
備的需求。
日本 7 月外國旅客入境人數為 344.2 萬人次，年增 0.1%。本月份外國旅客人數最大宗仍是來自

南韓，達 89.5 萬人，年增 31.9%。其次則為台灣遊客的 76.4 萬人次，年增 26.4%。與之相反，來
自中國的遊客人數年減幅達 56.1%， 因日中兩國關係遲遲未見改善所致 。 根據日本百貨店協會公布的
資料顯示，7 月百貨公司免稅銷售額年增 25.5%至 506 億日圓，連續五個月的正成長，顯示在日圓
延續弱勢的格局之下，外國旅客消費力仍有提升空間。
2026 年來日股 TOPIX 指數受惠於 AI 題材、日圓貶值、高市擴大財政刺激與強化軍事安全等政
策利多支撐，雖然日圓在政府干預與 BOJ 升息下繼續貶值空間受限，出口企業的匯兌紅利將逐步消
退，但預料日圓接下來仍以區間整理機會較高，並無大幅升值空間。目前 TOPIX 指數本益比約 17.7
倍、股價淨值比約 1.9 倍，評價仍屬合理，可利用市場震盪機會逢低分批布局。

台灣股市：工業生產創高、景氣續亮紅燈，AI 供應鏈撐起製造業擴張動能
8 月台股在 AI 龍頭財報優於預期及全球 AI 資本支出持續擴張帶動下，市場氣氛維持偏多，但受
到 Jackson Hole 會議後市場重新調整聯準會政策預期、美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強影響，指數於高
檔呈現震盪整理。資金面上，高評價 AI 族群漲多後出現輪動，市場開始重新檢視企業未來獲利成長
能否持續支撐評價水準。不過整體而言，AI 資料中心、半導體、先進封裝及伺服器供應鏈需求仍維
持強勁，有助於消化部分評價壓力，並使市場焦點重新回到企業基本面與獲利成長。

當前臺灣經濟仍處於景氣擴張階段。全球 AI 建置需求推動企業設備投資與科技資本支出維持高
檔，相關需求正逐步由晶片擴散至資料中心、網通設備、電力基礎建設及相關零組件。從國際環境觀
察，美國企業投資及私人需求維持韌性，AI 相關支出已成為支撐美國與全球科技景氣的重要力量，
也持續為台灣出口與半導體供應鏈提供結構性成長動能。
展望 9 月，市場關注焦點將由企業財報逐漸轉向總體經濟數據與聯準會政策方向。Jackson Hole
會議後，市場對於聯準會維持較高利率期間的預期升溫，短期可能提高全球金融市場波動。然而，從
企業基本面觀察，NVIDIA 最新展望顯示 AI 需求仍具高度能見度，全球雲端業者與大型企業的 AI 基
礎建設支出並未出現明顯放緩跡象。在此背景下，只要半導體、伺服器及先進封裝產業維持強勁接單
動能，台灣電子產業景氣仍有機會延續高檔表現。
投資策略方面，仍可維持對 AI 供應鏈的正向看法，但須更重視獲利成長與評價合理性。除了先
進製程及先進封裝外，AI 伺服器組裝、散熱、電源管理、高速傳輸、網通設備及記憶體等受惠 AI 基

建擴張之族群，仍具備中長期成長機會。隨著AI 投資逐步由 GPU 擴散至記憶體、網路交換器、光通
訊及系統整合等領域 ， 受惠範圍亦由少數龍頭供應商進一步擴展至整體供應鏈 ， 有助於支撐相關企業
未來營收與獲利成長動能。另一方面，9 月需留意美國 CPI 數據、聯準會利率決策及美債殖利率變化
對高評價科技股的影響；若市場因政策預期、資金輪動或短期獲利了結而出現回檔，較可能屬於多頭
趨勢中的震盪整理，而非基本面反轉。在企業獲利持續成長、AI 資本支出維持高檔及全球科技景氣
延續復甦的背景下 ， 建議布局方向可由單純題材面轉向具備技術門檻 、 訂單能見度及現金流品質較佳
的個股，以提升投資組合在景氣循環與市場波動中的穩健度。

中國股市：硬科技 IPO 熱潮延燒，A 股中報揭露「增收不增利」普遍現象
8 月中國經濟延續「總量承壓、科技產業相對強勢」的分化格局。儘管房地產調整與內需復甦力
道仍有限，但高端製造、人工智慧及數位經濟仍是政策資源與市場資金聚焦方向。在傳統產業景氣復
甦較為緩慢的情況下 ， 科技產業逐漸成為支撐經濟成長的重要動能 ， 顯示中國經濟正持續朝新舊動能
轉換與產業升級方向發展。
科技產業方面，人形機器人已成為近期最受市場關注的新興產業之一。根據中國國家市場監管總
局統計，2026 年上半年人形機器人領域新設企業數量達 11.6 萬家，較 2025 年同期的 7.2 萬家大幅

成長 61.1%，反映資本投入、技術研發與產業參與程度快速提升。隨著人工智慧、大模型及感測技術
進步，人形機器人正逐步由研發驗證邁向商業 化應用階段，產業發展重點也由概念題材轉向量產能
力、產品可靠度及終端需求驗證。
資本市場表現雖顯示科技板塊仍是 A 股資金主要聚焦方向 ， 但企業基本面改善幅度與市場期待之
間仍存在落差。從滬深 300 成分股來看，科技類企業近年營收維持增長，但獲利表現並未同步大幅
提升，反映部分企業仍面臨研發投入增加、產能擴張及市場競爭加劇等壓力。AI、半導體及高端製造
雖持續獲得政策支持與資本關注 ， 但不少企業的估值已提前反映未來成長預期 ， 實際獲利貢獻仍有待
進一步驗證。市場關注焦點也逐漸由政策題材與自主替代概念，轉向訂單能見度、現金流與獲利能力
等基本面指標；若後續營運表現未能跟上市場預期，不排除科技板塊仍面臨評價修正壓力。
展望 9 月，政策方向預計仍以穩增長與培育新質生產力為主軸，人工智慧、人形機器人、高端製
造及半導體等領域仍將是支持重點 。 在整體經濟復甦力道尚未全面擴散的背景下 ， 市場表現預期仍將

呈現結構性分化，資金持續聚焦具成長潛力與技術門檻的科技產業。投資策略上，可留意人形機器人
產業鏈、工業自動化、AI 應用、半導體設備及高端製造等受惠產業升級趨勢之族群；惟部分熱門題
材評價已反映較高成長預期，後續仍須觀察訂單落地、量產進度及獲利改善情況，避免因短期市場情
緒過熱而提高投資風險。

科技類股：AI 基礎建設持續擴張，算力、光通訊與平台生態同步受惠
全球科技產業的核心成長動能仍來自 AI 基礎建設擴張 ， 但市場焦點已逐步由單純GPU 採購轉向
整體資料中心架構升級 。 隨Google 擴大投入光通訊與高速網路技術 ，AI 資料中心正朝向更高頻寬、
更低延遲及更高能效方向發展 。 市場預估800G 至 1.6T 光通訊滲透率將持續提升 ， 推動光模組 、CPO、
交換器及相關供應鏈需求成長，也反映大型雲端服務供應商仍持續強化 AI 基礎建設布局。在此趨勢
下，AI 投資已由單一晶片擴展至網路、儲存、記憶體及先進封裝等完整生態系，帶動相關供應鏈重

新評價。
企業獲利方面，NVIDIA 最新財報再次驗證 AI 投資循環仍具延續性。公司第二季營收與資料中心
業務均大幅優於預期，資料中心網路業務亦維持高速成長，顯示大型企業、雲端服務業者及 AI 模型
開發商對算力需求仍然強勁。同時，管理層對第三季營收及新一代 Vera Rubin 平台維持正向展望，
反映 AI 基礎建設建置並未出現明顯放緩跡象。不過，市場對AI 產業的評價標準已逐漸由需求成長轉
向獲利品質與投資效益，未來資本支出、折舊攤提與現金流變化仍將是投資人關注重點。
從產業鏈角度來看，AI 需求正持續向外擴散。除了 GPU 之外，高速光通訊、網路交換器、高頻
寬記憶體(HBM)、CPU 及資料中心電力架構均成為下一階段重要受惠領域。另一方面，NVIDIA 近期
宣布結合金融機構與雲端服務資源打造大型 AI 基礎設施平台，顯示產業發展正由硬體銷售進一步延
伸至算力服務與平台營運模式。此舉有利強化 AI 產業生態系並提高資本取得效率，但也反映未來競
爭重點將由單純硬體性能逐步轉向平台整合能力、商業模式及資金運用效率。隨著更多企業投入 AI

建設，市場也開始關注高額資本支出是否能持續帶來相應回報，產業獲利表現仍可能出現明顯分化。
展望後續，AI 仍是科技產業最具確定性的投資主軸，但市場關注焦點正由 「建置多少算力」逐步
轉向「創造多少效益」。NVIDIA 新平台推廣進度、Vera Rubin 產品放量、高速光通訊滲透率提升
及 HBM 供給變化，將是觀察下一階段 AI 投資循環的重要指標。雖然大型雲端業者資本支出尚未出
現明顯下修，支撐整體供應鏈需求維持高檔，但隨著資料中心建置成本、能源支出與折舊費用持續增
加 ， 企業獲利能力與現金流表現的重要性亦將提升 。 未來較具競爭優勢的企業 ， 除了掌握核心技術外 ，
更需具備規模化商業模式及資本效率，才能在 AI 產業由高速成長邁向成熟發展的過程中持續創造價
值。

礦業類股：關稅預期有利銅價支撐，戰略庫存需求是潛在利多
8 月以來礦業類股走勢強勁，倫敦金屬交易所銅價也再創歷史新高，這波上漲的主要動能，仍是
來自於我們先前一再提到的美國潛在銅關稅，改變了全球銅庫存的流向 。 由於市場擔心美國未來可能
對精煉銅加徵關稅，交易商提前將銅運往美國，希望在關稅實施前完成進口，導致其他地區能夠取得
的現貨供應相應減少，在供給減少下持續為銅價帶來支撐。
截至 8 月底，美國現行關稅主要涵蓋銅棒、銅線、銅板及銅管等半成品與衍生產品，精煉銅尚未
正式納入課稅範圍，但市場普遍預估美國政府將有可能在 2027 年開始實施精煉銅關稅，帶動交易商

提前採取備貨行動。路透社報導指出，美國2026 年上半年進口約 88.5 萬噸精煉銅，年增約 3%，紐
約商品交易所銅庫存也攀升至約 67.5 萬噸的歷史高點。若將時間拉長至一年觀察，全球三大交易所
合計增加約 49.58 萬噸庫存，其中約 81%集中於紐約商品交易所，顯示全球可見銅庫存雖然上升，
但新增庫存大多被鎖在美國，非美市場銅庫存相對處於較為緊張的狀態。
除了關稅帶動的庫存 失衡外，銅的實際應用需求也具有基本面支撐。彭博行業研究 預估，2026
年全球銅需求將成長 2.4%至 2.7%，增速雖較 2025 年放緩，但電動車、再生能源、資料中心及電網
升級仍將帶動用銅需求上升。其中雖然中國銅需求成長預估也將放緩，但是中國國家電網已規劃 2026
至 2030 年投資 4 兆元人民幣，較前五年增加 44%，以支應人工智慧用電需求。
另一方面，隨著區域銅庫存出現短缺及 AI 帶動用銅需求上升，若政府與企業在未來計畫提高安
全庫存，將可能成為市場尚未充分反映的中長期上漲基礎。花旗研究估算，目前全球精煉銅總庫存約

為 300 萬噸，僅相當於 1.3 個月的全球消費量。若各國政府及大型供應鏈基於國防、能源安全、人
工智慧基礎建設或地緣政治風險 ， 將安全庫存提高至兩個月 ， 市場約需要額外累積 170 萬噸銅庫存；
若提高至三個月，額外需求更將擴大至約 400 萬噸。由於新礦山從開發到投產需要多年，供給無法
在短期內迅速增加，市場只能透過銅價上漲，促使更多廢銅進入市場，同時迫使部分使用者節約用銅
或尋找替代材料。因此，建立庫存的規模愈大、完成時間愈短，銅價需要調整的漲幅也將愈顯著。
展望後市，儘管美國精煉銅關稅目前尚未正式實施，但市場預期帶來的提前備貨潮仍有利短線銅
價支撐，同時全球用銅需求仍維持成長，新礦山供給又難以快速實現，加上政府及企業可能逐步提高
安全庫存 ， 使銅市場的中長期供需結構仍具有支撐 ， 故對具銅價曝險的礦業類股中長期後市仍可正向
看待 ， 然而仍需留意若未來銅關稅未如市場預期實施 ， 當前囤積於美國市場的銅庫存將可能回歸到全
球市場當中，為銅價帶來短線波動。

能源類股：荷莫茲海峽持續受阻，惟實際供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期
8 月以來，美伊衝突持續反覆升降溫，雙方時而釋出談判訊號，時而再度發生軍事衝突，短期內
仍未出現實質進展，荷莫茲海峽通行亦持續受限。然而，海峽受阻並不代表原有運量全數消失，部分
原油仍可經由海運與陸上替代管線出口 ，同時中國石油需求下降及各國政府釋放戰略庫存亦抵銷部分
中斷影響。
彭博行業研究指出，儘管當前荷莫茲海峽受到干擾，但實際並未呈現完全封鎖的狀態，目前仍有
零星油輪持續通行。彭博指出，由於部分油輪會關閉公開定位訊號，並在通過海峽後於阿曼外海進行

轉運，因此公開船舶資料可能會漏算部分運量。彭博綜合目前庫存變化與市場實際供需後，估計目前
每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油通過荷莫茲海峽，意味僅依據可觀察船舶數量推算中東石油供應中斷量，
可能高估了市場實際缺口。
除了每日仍有約 750 萬桶原油經過海峽運輸外，彭博估計目前另有每日約 500 萬桶石油改走陸
上管線；需求端則因中國每日減少約 400 萬桶進口而下降，其他地區減少用油與各國政府釋放戰略
庫存再合計填補每日約 200 萬桶石油缺口。經過上述調整後，原本每日約 2,000 萬桶經荷莫茲海峽
通行的運量中，實際供應缺口僅剩每日約 150 萬桶，這也解釋了當前油價漲幅為何未完全反映表面
的產量損失。
全球石油庫存變化的數據亦呼應了實際缺口低於市場普遍預期的論點。 花旗報告指出 ，2026 年 2
月至 8 月全球實際觀察到的石油供需缺口平均約為每日 290 萬桶，明顯低於其原先預估的每日 700
萬至 800 萬桶。截至 2026 年 8 月，全球石油庫存仍可覆蓋 99 天需求，亦仍處於 2022 年至 2025

年的 95 至 101 天區間。花旗估計，若目前庫存下降速度延續，全球庫存要到 2029 年第一季才會降
至接近第二次石油危機緊張程度的 70 天水位，這顯示荷莫茲海峽若持續受阻，雖油價上漲壓力仍會
逐步累積，但實際庫存下降速度並未如市場普遍預期的嚴峻。
展望後市， 除荷莫茲海峽中斷造成的 供應缺口與庫存降幅低於市場預期 外，國際能源署 也預估
2027 年全球石油需求將增加每日 240 萬桶 ， 供應則可能年增每日830 萬桶 ， 意味若中東產量與海峽
通行逐步恢復，供應回升速度將明顯快於需求，並帶動庫存回補，不利油價上漲趨勢，然而考量短線
地緣風險仍有利能源類股形成支撐，在美伊達成長期停火協議前，應中性看待能源類股後市表現。

黃金類股：央行買盤支撐中長期價格，惟升息預期帶動短線波動升高
8 月金價走勢先揚後抑。月初公布的美國就業數據偏弱，加上中國及全球黃金 ETF 買盤回升，使
市場一度降低近期升息預期，金價因而突破 7 月的整理區間。到了 8 月中旬，美國財政部宣布擴大
長天期公債回購，帶動長端殖利率與美元回落，進一步放大黃金漲勢；不過，月底公布的通膨數據仍
顯示物價壓力具有黏性 ， 聯準會主席華許在傑克森霍爾會議的態度也偏向鷹派 ， 使市場重新提高升息
預期，金價隨即自高檔回落。然而即使月底出現修正，金價在 8 月仍累積明顯漲幅。
支撐金價中長期走勢的核心力量，仍然來自世界各國央行為分散外匯儲備，持續增持黃金。世界

黃金協會指出，2026 年第 2 季全球央行淨購金量為 289 噸，累計 2026 年上半年約 345 噸。雖然上
半年央行購金量受到土耳其、俄羅斯等國調節部位影響，降至2022 年以來同期低點，但第 2 季購金
量已較第 1 季明顯回升，而且買盤仍分布於波蘭、中國、烏茲別克及哈薩克等多個市場，顯示各國央
行增持黃金的趨勢仍未出現轉變，且可觀察到各國央行對於逢低承接黃金的意願。
在央行需求之外，中國投資資金是本輪金價突破的重要邊際買盤。2026 年上半年中國黃金淨進
口量達 764 噸，較 2025 年同期增加 138%，其規模明顯高於中國人民銀行公布的增持量，顯示民間
與機構投資人也在提高黃金配置 。 當中國資金推動金價突破整理區間後 ， 美國商品期貨交易委員會的
資料也顯示 ， 管理基金黃金淨多單在 8 月持續增加 ， 使原本集中於亞洲的漲勢進一步獲得美國期貨資
金支持。
然而，投資資金參與程度提高，也使金價對利率與美元變化更加敏感。美國財政部 8 月 19 日宣
布，將至少加倍 10 年至 30 年期公債的流動性支持回購規模，市場隨即下調長端殖利率，黃金也在

持有成本下降後快速上漲。不過，月底公布的美國 7 月通膨數據仍高，加上華許在傑克森霍爾會議維
持偏鷹立場，再次使市場重新認為聯準會仍可能透過升息抑制通膨，美元與殖利率回升後，部分短線
資金便出現獲利了結賣壓。
展望後市，各國央行增持黃金儲備仍支撐黃金的中長期多頭結構，而美國財政與債務可持續性疑
慮也將持續提高黃金的分散風險價值。不過，8 月底的回檔顯示，當金價漲勢由 ETF 及期貨資金放大
後，實質利率與美元只要轉為上升，短線市場部位便可能迅速調整，故在美國後續利率政策仍具有不
確定性的狀況下，對於黃金類股後市應中性看待。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：長端風險仍存，3~10 年期公債、投資級債更具表現機會
8 月以來，美國公債殖利率維持高檔震盪，若以 7 月底與 8/24 比較，各年期債券殖利率近乎平
行小幅下行。8 月初公布的 7 月非農就業意外減少 2.3 萬人，顯示勞動市場動能轉弱；7 月 CPI 年增
率由 3.5%降至 3.4%，核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.6%降至 2.5%，一度緩解市場對 Fed 緊縮疑慮。惟 7
月 PPI 與 PCE 數據表現，限制利率進一步下行空間。
8 月中旬之後，市場焦點由政策利率逐步轉向能源價格、財政供給與期限溢酬。美伊局勢反覆推

升油價，加上美國聯邦債務8 月首度突破 40 兆美元，2 年與 30 年期利差由 7 月底 99 個基點擴大至
8/18 的 109 個基點，30 年期殖利率亦升至 5.28%。美國財政部 8/19 宣布，自 9/9 起將部分 10～
30 年期公債單次流動性回購上限由 20 億美元提高至至少 40 億美元，帶動 30 年期殖利率由 5.28%
快速回落至 5.19%。截至 8/24，2 年與 30 年利差亦回到 99 個基點，月中陡峭化大致回吐。此一走
勢顯示 ，回購措施雖有助改善長券流動性 ， 但財政赤字 、公債供給及期限溢酬等結構性壓力仍未消失 。
全球主要成熟國家的長端債券亦面臨類似壓力。德國 30 年期公債殖利率近期升至約 3.79%，創
2011 年以來最高；日本 10 年期公債殖利率 8/18 盤中一度觸及 2.945%，亦為 1996 年 9 月以來高
位，30 年期亦一度升至 4.115%。成熟市場長債同步承壓，反映財政擴張、通膨風險與貨幣政策正常
化正共同推升長端期限溢酬。

不過，信用市場整體仍相對穩定，截至 8/24，聖路易聯準銀行的 ICE 美國銀行美國投資級公司
債 OAS 約 81 個基點、有效殖利率約 5.4%，但信用利差仍處歷史偏低區間，緩衝並不寬裕。AI 基礎
建設融資的供給壓力亦已開始反映在定價，Reuters 8/21 指出，2026 年至 8 月上旬主要 AI 雲端業
者發債規模已約 2,200 億美元，科技類投資級債利差升至 89 個基點，較整體 IG 市場高出約 9 個基
點；近期 Alphabet 新券亦需提供約 10～15 個基點讓利，顯示在企業基本面仍穩健之際，大量新券
供給已成為影響信用評價的重要因素。
展望 9 月，美債殖利率預料仍將處於相對高檔，若就業持續降溫且通膨未再明顯升高，中期利率
上行空間可望受限。期限配置方面，短端對 Fed 政策預期較敏感，過短存續期間亦有再投資風險；
10 年以上債券則持續承受財政與期限溢酬壓力。值得注意的是，截至 8/27，10 年與 30 年期殖利率
利差為 52 個基點，2 年至 30 年總利差 96 個基點，顯示 2 年至 30 年期殖利率曲線陡峭幅度中，逾

半來自 10~30 年區間，反映超長端相對 10 年期仍有明顯期限溢酬。相較之下，3～10 年期兼具較
高息收、適度存續期間與利率下行參與空間。整體而言，目前 3～10 年期美國公債及投資級債的風
險報酬條件相對均衡，料具表現機會。

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

義隆 (2458 TT) 
受累終端需求疲軟

永豐觀點 
終端應用以筆電為重，受終端需求不確定性較大。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持中立 ：由於整體 PC 市況疲軟 ， 又義隆電本身模組產品也受記憶體與被動元件
短缺影響，加以製造成本攀升，使得公司營運承壓，研究部下修獲利預估值。研究
部預估義隆電 2026 年稅後 EPS 6.07 元(原預估 9.03 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 5.54
元(原預估 9.81 元)。義隆電因產品線集中筆電領域，市況仍顯弱勢，又製造成本上
升使毛利率承壓，維持中立建議。

ESG 評析 
義隆企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

### Page 2

2

義隆電為筆電觸控板與觸控螢幕晶片大廠 ：義隆電為全球筆電相關晶片大廠，其中
公司於觸控板市佔率>50%、觸控螢幕晶片市占率>50%、Pointstick 為市場主導
者，筆電指紋辨識市占率 30%~35%。義隆電產品結構 82%觸控相關，18%來自
MCU、Pointstick 與關係企業，公司產品線主要競爭對手為 Synaptics。義隆電晶
圓代工廠夥伴為聯電、台積電與 Magnachip。  
3Q26 獲利欠佳：義隆電 3Q26 營收 22.25 億元(-30.7%QoQ，-33%YoY)，毛利
率 44.7%，營業利益率 13.3%，稅後獲利 1.42 億元(-84%QoQ，-82%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 0.47 元，獲利欠佳。義隆電3Q26 各項財務細項如下：(1) 營收下滑顯著，
主要是客戶提前拉貨、 消費電子需求疲軟、 長短料不匹配而影響對義隆電晶片拉貨
(CPU 與記憶體為短料)。義隆電 3Q26 僅指紋辨識持平，其餘產品均下滑(AI 產品
線/觸控板滑落幅度較小)，觸控螢幕/指向裝置下滑幅度較大，公司 3Q26 產品結
構為觸控比重 73%(觸控板 43%/觸控螢幕 14%/指紋辨識 16%)、非觸控比重
13%(指向裝置 9%/MCU 與其他 4%)與 AI 產品線 14%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的
47.4%降至 44.7%，主要是(製造成本攀升)、產品結構轉差(高毛利率的觸控螢幕與
指紋辨識下滑較多)與被動元件/PCB 漲價，由於公司多項產品屬於模組形式，故毛
利率亦受此影響。(3) 業外損失 1 億元(前季認列業外收益 2.94 億元)，主要來自金
融資產評價損失。 
4Q26 營收仍有挑戰：由於 3Q26 業外損亦超逾稅前獲利 10%，故義隆電本次法
說未給予展望，而由 10/2026 營收僅 4.61 億元(-23%MoM，-54%YoY)，反映市
場整體需求仍顯弱勢。研究部預估義隆電 4Q26 營收 15.76 億元(-29%QoQ，-
45%YoY)，毛利率 44.9%，稅後獲利 1.17 億元(-18%QoQ，-84%YoY)，稅後 EPS 
0.39 元。 
受筆電市況疲軟衝擊 ：研究部認為義隆電主要業務受累於筆電市場疲軟， 使得營運
表現受波及，公司預期 2027 年以車載、無人機更有表現力道。(1) 車載導入電動
物流車與智慧交通。義隆電表示今年電動巴士成長翻倍，第六個客戶將於2027 年
步入小量量產，而若政府通過電動物流車補助措施，則預計2027 年亦將導入電動
物流車供應鏈 ， 體量將高於電動巴士 。(2) 義隆電無人機產品為相機模組(包括固定
焦距、變焦與夜視功能等)、AI box 與避障偵測，今年導入雷虎等客戶系統，明年
將參與歐美日展覽，期待營收進一步擴增。針對光通訊產品維持預計 EIC2H27 送
樣。義隆電藉由入股 PETA Optronics 15%~16%，而導入光通訊研發，義隆電將
與 PETA 共同設計開發電晶片(包括 TIA 阻抗放大器、雷射驅動晶片等)，處理高頻

高速的電子電路與電訊號傳輸，預計 2H27 送樣。針對 Synaptics 被 On-Semi 收

## Document 116: 261104_daiwa_guc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261104_daiwa_guc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following its results release on 30 October, we raise our 
forecasts and 12M TP for Global Unichip (GUC). The stock more than 
doubled since our last update (Upgrading: unleashed capacity adding to 
growth, 5 August), yet its recent pullback amid increased market volatility 
looks to be unlocking value again for investment, so we reaffirm our Buy (1) 
rating. Fundamentals have unfolded strongly. According to our market 
research, GUC’s key customer in the AI CPU space has pulled forward its 
order trajectory ahead of the previous schedule amid accelerated demand 
for internal and external AI datacentre build-out. This comes on top of its 
initial ramp of another new accelerator project, followed by the next 
generation AI CPU and accelerator ramps for the same customer, with 
further upside from another new AI accelerator customer in the auto space. 
We suggest investors take advantage of the pullback to bargain-hunt.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. In light of its key AI CPU customer 
accelerating its ordering trajectory, together with multiple AI projects 
ramping in the next 1-2 years to fire on all cylinders for growth, we lift our 
2026-28E EPS by 8-50% for GUC. We forecast triple-digit % YoY EPS 
growth over 2Q26-2Q27, before moderating to 50% or so into 2028. While 
such strong growth should help sustain its hefty valuation, further rerating 
may be unlikely as this would depend on GUC’s ability to win more AI 
projects than we expect, and more importantly, whether it is able to secure 
enough capacity from the supply chain to accommodate any incremental 
business amid the constrained supply environment.

Margin-accretive at operating level. On top of the AI CPU demand uplift, 
we expect GUC to have a total of 6 major AI projects in mass-production for 
2027, including 4 CPU and 2 accelerators, all for US-based hyperscalers or 
system vendors mainly focusing on N3 process nodes, helping broaden 
GUC’s AI project base. Although these projects appear margin-dilutive at 
gross level, they should be accretive at the operating level, thanks to 
GUC’s prudent opex management on enhanced operating leverage.

What we recommend: Given our forecast changes, we lift our 12M TP for 
GUC to TWD7,800 (previous: TWD5,125), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 67x (previous: 63x), justified by its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 66% from a PEG perspective. Although further rerating remains 
challenging, we are comfortable with our Buy (1) call due to the recent 
share price pullback unlocking value for investment again. Key downside 
risks to our call would be supply constraints and margin execution.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E EPS are 23-37% below the consensus 
forecasts as we factor in the supply constraints that will moderate growth.

4 November 2026

Value returns when jitters rise

➢ Lifting forecasts on AI strength and a broadened project base 
➢ Margin dilutive at gross level yet accretive at operating level 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) with a higher 12M TP of TWD7,800

Company profile 
Founded in 1998, GUC is an IC design service provider focusing on AI/HPC, networking, consumer and 
industrial applications.

### Page 5

5

Global Unichip (3443 TT): 4 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
GUC's Board of Directors (BoD) comprises 8 members, including 4 independent directors (50%
of the board), which is significantly higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. The
Board demonstrates diverse expertise across legal, finance, technology, and business
management. The Chairman and the President are different individuals, ensuring separation of
roles and enhancing governance independence.
Capital management 1
Over the past decade, GUC has maintained a consistently high dividend payout ratio, generally
above 60%, and in most years exceeding 80%. In 2025, the company distributed TWD2,144m in
cash dividends, reflecting its policy of sustaining or increasing dividend amounts over time.
Given GUC’s asset-light business model as an IC design service provider, which does not
require significant capital expenditure for manufacturing facilities, this high payout ratio is
appropriate and aligns with its long-term commitment to shareholder returns.
Related party &
transaction 1
GUC's sole foundry supplier is TSMC, who is also GUC's largest shareholder. As an IC design
service company without onsite production, GUC's relationship with the leading foundry of
advanced process nodes provides it a key competitive advantage, in our view.
S Supply chain management 2
GUC is committed to maintaining long-term partnerships with suppliers and establishing a
sustainable supply chain through measures such as promoting its green action policy and
concept to the entire supply chain, where all its suppliers are required to follow its Responsible
Business Alliance (RBA) regulation. It also periodically conducts supplier audits to ensure the
quality of suppliers.
E Water & wastewater management 1
GUC continues its green living project to reduce water consumption. In 2024, the Company
maintained water-saving measures such as rainwater collection systems, automatic sensor
faucets, dual-flush toilets, and regular inspections of water equipment. These efforts aim to
improve water efficiency and promote conservation across operations.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
As an IC design service provider, GUC does not have in-house manufacturing facilities. It
requires its suppliers to use recycled materials for on-site operation and transportation, and
reduce the amount of expendable resources being used. In order to promote a paperless office,
GUC has established a cloud operating system to reduce the use of paper-based documents.
E GHG emissions 1
GUC adopts energy-saving measures to reduce greenhouse gas emissions and has set annual
electricity conservation targets. In 2024, the Company achieved a 1% reduction in electricity use
and increased green electricity to 39% of total consumption. It also continues to develop low-
power and high-efficiency IP to help minimise environmental impact.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
3
AI infrastructure demand, supply constraints and 
engineering productivity are key for growth outlook
Despite rising concerns over data center power availability, infrastructure 
deployment delays and potential AI investment moderation, management reported 
no meaningful slowdown in customer demand or changes in development 
schedules. Based on discussions with major customers, Alchip expects strong 
demand extending through 2027–2029, with next-generation AI accelerator 
volumes potentially exceeding current-generation levels substantially. 
Management cautioned that shipment delays could arise from supply-side 
constraints, particularly PCB, substrates, passive components and advanced 
semiconductor capacity, rather than weakening end demand. More importantly,  
large AI accelerator projects generally receive priority allocation, leaving smaller 
customers and traditional applications more exposed to shortages. Meanwhile, AI 
adoption is materially improving Alchip’s engineering productivity, particularly in 
RTL coding, verification, design iteration and analysis of implementation reports. 
Alchip’s CPU and networking projects are progressing on schedule, but 
management believes advanced AI accelerator designs offer substantially greater 
engineering differentiation and revenue opportunities than conventional CPU 
projects.
Looking into 2028, the key limitation could shift from customer demand to Alchip’s 
ability to finance and support larger wafer procurement commitments. 
Consequently, working-capital requirements, customer payment terms and the 
potential need for additional financing will become increasingly important factors 
in determining the company’s achievable revenue scale and profitability. 
We lift our 2027 /2028 earnings projection for Alchip by 14% and 13%, reflecting 
stronger order allocation into next two years. We are maintaining our Buy on the 
stock but lowering our TP to NT$6,000, with lower PER at 27x of 2027 EPS (from 
35x previously), reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty, in the low end of its 
historical PER.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,000 is based on 27x 2027E EPS. 27x target
PER is lower than its historical 3-year PER range, which we see as justified on
account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with its key CSP
client, reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty but with better project cycle
and strong growth momentum resuming next year. Our target price equates
to 2026/27E P/B of 6x/5x.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
October  Sales +14% MoM/
+30% YoY
In this report, we focus on Delta's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• October sales came in at NT$74,666mn (+14% MoM/+30% YoY), driven by
the increasing shipment of power supply units, HVDC power racks, and liquid
cooling products.
• QTD revenue reached 35% of MSe at NT$215,881mn (+10% QoQ/+34% YoY),
and 34% of consensus at NT$219,806mn (+12% QoQ/+36% YoY).

Our view:
• We think 4Q26 revenue is tracking slightly ahead of market expectations.
• We think the slightly better Oct monthly sales should help on near-term
sentiment, though more meaningful upside to market expectations on
earnings growth might  depend on the penetration of HVDC power racks into
the coming years.
• We continue to see Delta as a key beneficiary of the increasing power
consumption of AI clusters through its comprehensive power/cooling

offerings. Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Delta Electronics Inc. (2308.TW)
Base case: P/E multiple of 30x based on average 2027-28e EPS.                          We believe our target multiple
is justified by the 59% earnings CAGR we estimate for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.5, versus
the stock's ~23x P/E and 23% earnings CAGR during 2022-25, which implied a PEG of 1.0.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected global economic growth

n Falling raw material prices
n Margin improvement
Risks to Downside
n Global economic slowdown
n Ongoing component supply tightness
n Rising raw material prices
n Margin contraction as a result of higher operating expenses or faster ASP erosion

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 175: 261110_daiwa_m31

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_m31.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following on our results Memo, we lower our forecasts and 
TP for M31, as we factor in its still suboptimal scale and delayed 
employment bonus in 3Q26 that weigh on its trajectory of a bottom-line 
turnaround. That said, we remain comfortable with our Buy (1) call on the 
stock as we expect M31’s turnaround, kicking off from 3Q26 despite at a 
small scale, to sustain into 2027 with meaningful incremental builds, 
supported by its conversion from license to royalty revenue paying off, and 
broadened footprint into advanced technologies for price accretion and into 
diversified foundry, IDM, and fabless customers for volume accretion.

What’s the impact: Forecast changes. We lower our 2026-27E EPS for 
M31 by 14-43% to embed its still suboptimal scale and opex overhang in 
our model. Despite the cuts, its bottom-line turnaround met our expectation, 
and we expect the turnaround to sustain with meaningful incremental builds 
sequentially through 2027 to improve market confidence. We forecast its 
4Q26 top line to rebound by 23% QoQ (+12% YoY) for EPS to reach 
TWD3.31 (TWD0.35 in 3Q26), setting a good start for M31’s 2027 outlook 
and warranting a strong 213% EPS CAGR over 2026-28E.

Turnaround confirmed. While prolonged license project recognitions 
delayed M31’s turnaround, depicted in our previous Flash, we believe its 
bumpy operations over the past 2 years is finally coming to an end, 
evidenced by management’s comments of QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26 
with EPS expanding to cover accumulated losses YTD. M31 has actively 
invested over the past years enriching its IP portfolio, accumulating more 
than 2,000 license projects since 2012 (chart on p.2), and setting a good 
foundation for converting them to royalty revenue. It aims for the royalty 
revenue to outgrow license revenue in the next few quarters, after years of 
“cooking”. Meanwhile, two structural growth drivers remain intact for ASP 
and volume accretion: 1) for ASP, a growing number of high-value projects 
from advanced process nodes to enhance M31’s IP portfolio for ASP 
accretion, and 2) for volume, an increased diversification of customer base 
globally to broaden its addressable markets for volume accretion.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lower our 12M TP 
for M31 to TWD550 (previous: TWD645), still based on our 4-quarter 
forward PER of 45x. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with key downside risks 
of silicon inflation and geopolitical overhang leading to global inflation and 
AI capex cut to delay M31’s royalty ramps.

How we differ: Our 2027E EPS is 52% above the consensus, likely due to 
our more positive view on royalty revenue ramp.

10 November 2026

Structural turnaround round the corner (III)

➢ Lowering our forecasts to reflect still suboptimal scale… 
➢ … but expecting enriched license projects to bear fruit soon 
➢ Reiterating our Buy (1) rating with a lower 12M TP of TWD550

Company profile 
Founded in 2011, M31 is an independent semiconductor IP provider in Taiwan, offering both foundation 
IP for specialty technology nodes and functional IP such as high-speed interface and analogue IP.

### Page 5

M31 Technology (6643 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 11 Mar 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
M31's board of directors (BoD) has 7 members, including 3 independent directors, who
represent 43% of total directors, higher than the regulatory requirement of at least 20%. Its
Chairman and President are not the same person, but its President is one of the directors. M31
has the Audit Committee, Compensation Committee and Risk Control Committee under the
BoD.
Capital management 1
Although M31 is a young company (founded in 2011 and listed in 2019), its dividend payout ratio
has been over the 75% level for the past 5 years. M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without
onsite production activities, which would require high capex to do so. As such, we view its high
dividend payout ratio to be appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 We do not see any meaningful impact from related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 2
M31's suppliers are required to comply with the Supplier Management Standards, which was set
by M31 in 2013. In addition, M31 has periodical evaluations for its suppliers and onsite audits to
ensure the quality and implementation of Corporate Social Responsibility (CSR), as well as
Environmental Protection and Safety and Integrity Policies.
E Water & wastewater management 1 M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production activity to affect the environment.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
The company is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production
activity to affect the environment. It still adopts some measures to reduce waste from its
operations, such as recycling and using electronic files to replace paper documents as much as
possible to reduce the use of paper.
E GHG emissions 1 M31 is a semiconductor IP provider without physical products, and thus no production activity to affect the environment.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

## Document 186: 261110_ms_gce

### Page 1

M
Idea
See our 3Q26 results recap: 3Q26 Results Summary
4Q preview – revenue growth and margin expansion: We estimate 4Q revenue
will rise ~8% q/q, to NT$31.8bn (+94% y/y), driven by higher ASPs and shipments
deferred from Sep – Oct following flood-related logistics disruptions in Thailand.
We expect sequential revenue growth across all major product segments except PC/
NB, with networking delivering the largest increase, followed by AI/ASIC servers and

general servers. We forecast margin expansion of ~100bps q/q, to 39.7% (+600bps
y/y), supported by continued mix improvement and pricing adjustments to pass
through higher copper-clad laminate (CCL) costs. This should translate into net
profit of ~NT$7bn (+9% q/q, +130% y/y) and EPS of NT$13.82.
GCE’s Suzhou 2 and Thailand 2 plants remain on track to begin mass production
(MP) from 3Q27 and reach full ramp-up by end-CY27. Both facilities will support

advanced high-density interconnect (HDI) production for a key customer’s next-gen
compute board, which we think could adopt a 34-layer (3+28+3) advanced HDI
design using M9-grade CCL. While details remain subject to finalization, we expect
each plant to add monthly output value of ~NT$1bn or more. We also expect at
least one additional plant to begin advanced HDI MP in CY28, although details have
yet to be finalized. This would give GCE at least three plants capable of advanced

HDI production to meet demand from existing ASIC, and potential new, customers.
Reiterate OW with PT slightly lowered to NT$1,400, on our reduced medium-term
growth assumption (from 12% to 11%), as we bake in some conservatism on
concerns over a potential slowdown in data center build-out post the US midterm
election (see our Global Team's view), despite our marginally higher EPS estimates
for 2026-28e. Our new PT implies ~19x 2027e P/E, or ~14x 2028e P/E, slightly

lowered but still above GCE's 3Y P/E avg. of ~14x, which we view as fair, driven by its
capacity expansion plans and strong demand for AI servers and networking, with
room for margin expansion.

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
3Q26 Results Summary
• GCE reported 3Q26 gross margin of 38.7% (+400bp q/q, +310bp y/y), 180bp above
MSe and 190bps above the Street forecast. Management attributed the gross
margin improvement to successful cost pass-through, more favorable product mix
(AI mix was over 50% in the quarter; 800G switch mix also continued to increase
within Networking) and better profitability of the Thailand plant.
• Recall that we noted although GCE is able to fully pass through the additional

material costs to its customers, there will always be a timing difference (GCE
seeing higher input costs vs. reflecting the higher costs in new prices), which might
result in margin fluctuation.
• Operating expenses were again higher than expected, primarily due to higher
employee bonuses for talent retention and sales expenses. Thus, operating profit
came in at NT$8.8B (+36% q/q, +88% y/y), in line with MSe but 1% below
consensus forecasts, for operating margin of 29.6%.

• Non-operating losses of NT$238mn, primarily consisting of FX losses, were higher
than our and Street estimates of non-operating gains.
• Net profit was NT$6.4B (+33% q/q, +98% y/y), 2% above our estimate and    the
Street, for EPS of NT$12.39.
• The differences between net profit variances and EPS variances reflect share count
changes.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
We lower our price target slightly, to NT$1,400.00 from NT$1,450.00: We derive our
price target, which is also our base-case scenario value, from a residual income (RI) model,
similar to the rest of our tech hardware coverage. The decrease in our price target is
driven by our slightly lowered medium-term growth assumption.
Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of

8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 11% (lowered from 12%)
and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our price target implies ~19x 2027e P/E, or ~14x 2028e P/E, vs. GCE's three-year historical
average 14.2x. We view the higher multiple as justified because GCE is showing longer-
term margin expansion, driven by a favorable mix shift toward more AI server PCBs and
high-speed networking PCBs (400G/800G/1.6T) alike. Some of its AI component peers are

trading at 20x+ 2027e multiple, which leads us to view GCE's valuation as fair.
Our bull- and bear-case scenario values decrease at  magnitudes similar to the decline
in our base-case value, to NT$1,995.00 and NT$850.00, respectively.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아 쉬 웠 습 니 다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추 정 치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

《⼤空头》原型 Steve Eisman 强调，与 AI 相关资产占标普 500 逾 65% 权重。他拆解四种市
场前景，认为有 70% 概率出现回撤或熊市。五⼤核⼼⻛险涵盖：云巨头被迫 " 重资产化 " 、电
⼒与审批的物理瓶颈、极端客⼾集中度、 OpenAI 财务隐患，以及最关键的，模型间⼏乎为
零的转换成本引发价格战。
摘要：
当⾼达 8500 亿美元的 AI 资本开⽀⾼度系于极少数缺乏⻓期护城河的模型公司时，华尔街传奇空
头警告，⼀旦现实不及预期，不仅市场将⾯临⼤规模回调，美国经济也极易被推⼊衰退。
11 ⽉ 9 ⽇，《⼤空头》原型之⼀、前 Neuberger Berman 基⾦经理 Steve Eisman 在播客《 The
Real Eisman Playbook 》上线了⼀期 40 分钟的单⼈播客。
⾯对当下占据绝对主导地位的 AI 投资狂潮， Steve Eisman 并未质疑 AI 改变世界的潜⼒，但他将
审视的⽬光投向了商业本质：巨⼤的资本⽀出由谁买单？增⻓是否可持续？以及最终谁能真正

获利？
Steve Eisman 在开场直击核⼼：
节⽬上线前四天，美国中期选举⺠主党拿下参众两院。 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇标普 500 跌 4.4% 、纳指跌 5.5% ，
电⼒、芯⽚与数据中⼼设备个股普遍跌 7% 到 17% 。 11 ⽉ 8 ⽇英伟达董事会宣布对⻩仁勋启动独
⽴调查，本周两⽇股价累跌 10% 。
⾯对今年单是超⼤规模云⼚商就⾼达 8500 亿美元的 AI 基础设施资本开⽀， Steve Eisman 围绕产
业链的偿付关系，拆解了四种市场情景与五⼤核⼼⻛险。
65% 的标普权重与 70% 的回撤概率
Eisman ⾸先指出， AI 早已不仅是科技板块的主题，⽽是整个市场的定价变量。他算了⼀笔账：
AI 已经成为当今投资界的主导主题。它驱动着股市，影响着债市，并在很⼤程度上驱动着整个美
国经济。美国很容易陷⼊衰退，我们⼏乎肯定还会看到⼀次⼤规模的市场回调。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

Eisman 强调：
甚⾄在债券市场，今年新发⾏的公司债中也有超过 50% 与 AI 相关。他强调：
在此基础上， Eisman 对 AI 叙事的未来给出了四种情景预测：
综合来看， Eisman 认为有 70% 的概率市场将⾯临某种形式的回撤或熊市。
巨头被迫 “ 重资产化 ” ，表外融资幽灵重现
在具体的⻛险拆解中， Eisman 将第⼀⼤⻛险指向了出钱⽅ —— 超⼤规模云⼚商的资产负债表。
他认为：
信息技术板块占标普 500 指数 40% ，加上⾕歌、亚⻢逊等 “ 科技属性 ” 公司，⽐例达到 50% ；
再算上今年为 AI 投⼊ 2500 亿美元的公⽤事业板块，占 3% ，以及⼯业板块中建设数据中⼼和供
电的公司，如伊顿、 GE Vernova ；
最后加上为 AI 融资的⾦融机构。
与 AI 相关的权重远远超过标普 500 的 65% 。
据我判断，完全不涉及 AI 的只有两个板块，必需消费品和医疗保健，两者加起来只占标普的
14% 。
整个市场不仅直接或间接地和 AI 相关，市场⾛向取决于 AI 未来的健康和成功。

⽜市情景（ 30% 概率）：  AI 持续改变世界，模型公司和英伟达不断推⾼股市。
熊市情景（ 30% 概率）：  采⽤率缓慢，价格战导致回报不及预期，最终引发全⾯衰退、市场
崩盘及私募信贷市场平仓。
中间情景（ 40% 概率）：  数据中⼼建设远超需求。他引⽤ PAA Research 分析师的估算，到
2027 年底美国数据中⼼产能将达到需求的两倍，导致资本开⽀在 2028 年放缓。 “ 结果是市场
回吐⼤部分 AI 涨幅，并出现⼀段时间的熊市。 ”
末⽇情景（ 0% 概率）：  对于机器接管地球的科幻叙事，他⼀针⻅⾎地指出： “他们兜售这套说
法，顺着⾃⼰制造出来的恐惧情绪⾛，⽬的是吓住政府，⽤监管把竞争清出市场。”
AI 改变了游戏规则。归根结底，AI 资本开⽀的需求把超⼤规模⼚商的商业模式从轻资本改成了重
资本。

⼀座⽀持 1 吉⽡电⼒需求的数据中⼼造价⾼达 300 亿美元。过去拥有庞⼤⾃由现⾦流⽤于回购股
票的巨头，如今正承受巨⼤的融资压⼒。

⽂过去两年总债务从 940 亿激增⾄ 1550 亿美元，评级被下调⾄仅⽐垃圾级⾼⼀档的 BBB- 。

虽然这些财务操作不⾜以单独击沉 AI 叙事，但 “ 它只是让这件事变得更危险。 ”

家。 Eisman 强调：
顺着这个逻辑， Eisman 将第四个⻛险直接锁定在 OpenAI ⾝上。他直⾔：

尽管是 Meta 在建这个数据中⼼，也承诺租 20 年、并承担项⽬延期的成本，债务却不会落在 Meta

英伟达要么投资、要么借钱给别的科技公司，那些公司再拿这笔钱来买英伟达的芯⽚。
AI 数据中⼼建不起来， AI 故事就不可能成⽴。
英伟达的 10-Q ⾥写着，其应收账款的 70% 来⾃五个客⼾，⽽应收账款是收⼊的代理指标。
因此只能得出⼀个结论：美国经济的健康极度依赖超⼤规模⼚商的资本开⽀，⽽超⼤规模⼚商的

在这两家之间， OpenAI 是更弱的那个。

甲⻣⽂。

下会把美国⽴刻推⼊衰退。
AI 周围似乎不存在任何护城河。模型之间的转换成本看起来也许是零，但肯定⾮常低。我没法把
这条⻛险的重要性说过头。
Anthropic 和 OpenAI 的资产负债表承受不住⼀场价格战。

## Document 289: Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超

Source PDF: all report pdf/Anthropic IPO路演之际，OpenAI也披露财务数据：Q3收入不及，亏损远超.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

OpenAI 三季度营收达 100 亿美元，季环⽐增⻓近 50% ，年化运⾏速率约 500 亿美元，但运
营亏损仍从 101 亿扩⼤⾄ 109 亿美元。竞争对⼿ Anthropic 启动 IPO 路演，三季度营收 170 亿
美元，亏损仅 5.67 亿，在体量与盈利能⼒上均领先。
摘要：
OpenAI 最新财务数据显⽰，其营收增⻓势头强劲，但盈利⽬标依然遥远。
11 ⽉ 10 ⽇，据彭博社援引知情⼈⼠透露，OpenAI 三季度（截⾄ 9 ⽉末）营收达 100 亿美元，季
环⽐增⻓近 50% ，同⽐增幅接近三倍。
调整后运营利润率从 -150% 则从改善⾄ -109% 。尽管利润率有所提升，但绝对亏损⾦额仍⼩幅扩
⼤，从 101 亿美元升⾄ 109 亿美元。
上述财务数据于周⼆在⼀场投资者电话会议中披露。CFO Sarah Friar 将这⼀季度定性为 " 爆炸
性季度 " ，并透露公司年化营收运⾏速率已达约 500 亿美元。
竞争对⼿ Anthropic 正于同⽇启动 IPO 路演。据 Anthropic 此前披露，其第三季度营收已达 170 亿

美元，同期运营亏损仅 5.67 亿美元，⽆论在体量还是盈利能⼒上均⼤幅领先 OpenAI 。
OpenAI 营收⾼增⻓难掩亏损扩⼤，继续加码企业市场
据 Sarah Friar 介绍，三季度营收增⻓的核⼼驱动⼒来⾃前沿研究和企业业务。
她表⽰， OpenAI 企业业务单季度实现翻倍增⻓，推动企业端与消费者端收⼊⽐例趋近于五五
开。在具体产品层⾯， Friar 点名了编程⼯具 Coding 、⽹络安全产品 Cyber 以及企业 AI 智能体平台
Dots 。她说：
中⼩企业业务同样录得加速，三季度增速超过此前六个季度的总和。然⽽，运营亏损从 101 亿美
元⼩幅上升⾄ 109 亿美元，表明公司在控制成本⽅⾯仍⾯临较⼤压⼒。
OpenAI 正将更多资源押注于利润率更⾼的企业市场，以缩⼩与 Anthropic 之间的差距。Friar 坦
⾔， Anthropic 在企业端的成功为 OpenAI 提供了重要的战略参照。
她表⽰，此前公司在微软 Azure 合同框架内受到约束，限制了与第三⽅云服务商开展分发合作的

空间。 Friar 说：
为强化这⼀战略，公司近期任命 Dali Rajic 出任⾸席营收官，专职负责企业业务板块的拓展。企
业市场在 OpenAI 整体营收中的占⽐正持续提升。
正是这些产品在企业端加速落地，推动了业绩增⻓。
我认为将这⼀限制解开之后， Anthropic 向我展⽰了这种模式的强⼤⼒量，对我们⽽⾔，这在
2027 年可以成为⾮常重要的增⻓顺⻛。

消费者端⽅⾯，Friar 披露消费者业务季环⽐销售额提升 20% ，付费订阅⽤⼾数突破 1 亿。公司整
体每周活跃⽤⼾达 12 亿。值得注意的是，与 OpenAI 不同， Anthropic 不设消费端业务，增⻓完全
依赖企业市场。
推出商业主⻚功能，剑指⾕歌本地搜索
在产品层⾯，华尔街⻅闻提及，周⼆公司宣布推出 "Businesses in ChatGPT" 测试版，允许企业
认领并管理其在 ChatGPT 上的商业主⻚，追踪⽤⼾互动数据，并通过专属 AI 代理与潜在客⼾直
接对话。
符合条件的企业还可创建专属的业务代理（ Business Agent ），使 ChatGPT ⽤⼾能够直接向其提
问，获取关于该企业及其产品的信息。
此外，商业中⼼还将向企业提供有限度的数据洞察，涵盖个⼈主⻚浏览量、品牌提及次数、⽹

站点击量等指标，这⼀设计令⼈联想到⾕歌分析（ Google Analytics ）的功能定位。
在验证机制⽅⾯，企业须经过正式流程完成⾝份核验。 OpenAI 货币化副总裁 Benji Shomair 表
⽰，公司针对不同市场、不同类型的企业采⽤多种验证⽅式，但未披露具体细节。
双雄竞速上市，估值⻆⼒进⼊关键窗⼝
融资与上市进程为两家公司的竞争增添了新的维度。
华尔街⻅闻提及， Anthropic 已于周⼆启动 IPO 路演，计划上市时间为 11 ⽉ 19 ⽇或 20 ⽇，⽬标估
值约为 1.4 万亿美元。
OpenAI 则选择将 IPO 计划推迟⾄明年，⽬前正以 1.25 万亿美元估值推进新⼀轮融资，已完成⾸
批 110 亿美元的股权募集。公司表⽰，所筹资⾦将⽤于⽀撑持续⾼强度的资本开⽀需求。
尽管两家公司在营收确认⽅式上存在差异，但三季度的业绩对⽐显⽰出明显的收敛态势 ——
Anthropic 以约 170 亿美元的绝对营收保持领先， OpenAI 则以更快的加速度弥合差距。
在各⾃ IPO 叙事成型的关键节点，这场营收竞赛的⾛势将直接影响投资者对两家公司⻓期商业潜

⼒的定价判断。

## Document 325: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-12).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

## Document 349: EcoNews of 9 November 2026 (2026-11-09)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 9 November 2026 (2026-11-09).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
In brief. The week was dominated by large market swings triggered by
the US midterms results. In the end, energy prices end the week much
lower, bond yields somewhat lower and stock prices down in tech-heavy
markets and slightly up elsewhere, notably in Europe. Incoming data
confirmed a picture of resilient growth overall. The Bank of England

hiked its policy rate by 25 bps and signaled it could do more. Market
pressure on France eased somewhat.
EUROZONE
Energy pushes inflation up, but underlying pressures remain rela -
tively contained. Headline inflation rose to 4.0% y/y in October, its
highest in three years, strengthening the case for another ECB rate
increase in December. Energy remained the main driver, while core in-
flation rose by 2.6%. The ECB wage tracker points to negotiated wage

growth slowing to 2.3% by September 2027. Meanwhile, retail sales fell
0.7% m/m in September and the average interest rate on new business
loans increased 13bp to 3.90%.
To watch next week: September trade balance, Q3 GDP second esti -
mate and employment (Friday).
France
EVs boost activity, market pressure eases somewhat. After already
bolstering household consumption in Q3, new car registrations rose
7% y/y in October (+4% y/y YTD), driven by another surge in electric

car sales (+81% y/y in October; +78% y/y YTD). In parallel, manufac -
turing output rebounded by 0.5% m/m in September, supported by
the automotive sector (+7.5% m/m but -5.5% y/y) and capital goods
(+2.6% m/m and +4.8% y/y). However, industrial production fell by
0.5% m/m (and 1.3% y/y), as a result of a decline in electricity produc-
tion (-4.2% m/m). The composite October PMI was revised down by
1.8 points from the flash estimate to 46.6 (-4.5 m/m), dragged down by

services. This sharp downgrade likely reflects the hit to sentiment from
the October 17 riots; however, precedents suggest activity itself should
not be visibly impacted. Appropriately indifferent to the meandering
journey of the draft budget through Parliament, the OAT-bund spread
narrowed to 155 bps (-10 bps in one week) amid a broad-based decline
in government bond yields across advanced economies; Thursday’s
long-term debt auction, the last of the year, was well-subscribed. Stri-

ving to meet its deficit target of 5.4% of GDP in 2026, the government
announced EUR3.8bn of spending cuts to offset EUR3.7bn in additional
expenditure. The High Council for Public Finance nevertheless sees the
deficit reaching 5.5%. The 2027 budget will start being discussed by
the Senate from next week, in its original version, after the national
Assembly unanimously rejected its amended version that would have
led to a 5.9% deficit.
To watch next week: Q3 unemployment rate and the Banque de France

monthly business survey (Tuesday).
Germany
Industrial activity picks up. New orders rebounded 4.4% m/m in Sep -
tember and industrial production increased 0.7%, with the auto sec -
tor delivering particularly striking gains of 10.5% in orders and 17.1%
in production. Mechanical-engineering orders also recovered (+8.3%),
while pharmaceutical production increased by 8.0%. By contrast, large
orders in aerospace, shipbuilding, rail and defence fell by 10.4%, while

machinery and equipment production declined by 6.5%. October's com-
posite PMI was revised up to 53.4, signaling continued strong expan -
sion.
Italy
Services keep the economy above water as manufacturing slips back.
The composite PMI eased to 50.8 in October, its lowest since May.
Manufacturing returned to contraction at 49.4, its weakest reading
since January, while services strengthened to 52.0 and recorded a fifth
consecutive month of growth. Both sectors reported increasing input-

cost and output-price inflation.
UNITED KINGDOM
The BoE hikes and leaves the door open to more. The Bank of England
raised Bank Rate by 25bp to 4%, its first increase since August 2023, in a
more hawkish than expected 8-1 vote, with Catherine Mann favouring
a 50bp hike. The November Monetary Policy Report sees CPI inflation
peaking around 5% y/y by mid-2027 and upgraded Q4 2026 growth to
2.2%, while cutting the 2027 and 2028 growth projections. The com -

posite PMI meanwhile improved to 52.9, its highest since February,
with new business growing at its strongest pace in nine months and
private-sector employment increasing for the first time in 25 months.
To watch next week: Q3 GDP , business investment, industrial produc-
tion (Thursday).
UNITED STATES
Post election market jitters and a hot jobs report. The Democrats’
unexpectedly sweeping victory in the midterms (the largest wave
since 1974) initially led markets to rally on (i) relief that the President

recognised the results, and (ii) hopes of a swift end to the Iran war
(oil prices and bond yields down, stock prices up). But this was soon
followed by a sharp equity sell-off on 5 November, with the S&P 500
down 4.4% and the Nasdaq down 5.0%, erasing USD2.7 Trillion of mar-
ket capitalization in one day. Why? Because of the realization that vo -
ters strongly supported AI-restrictive measures on the ballot (46 out
of 47) and candidates — for Congress and Governor seats — in favour

of curtailing data centers buildout. By the week-end, however, senti -
ment had turned around again, helped in part by a much stronger than
expected jobs report: payrolls increased by 165,000 in October against
a 70,000 consensus, while the previous two months were revised hi -
gher and unemployment edged down to 4.1%. Average hourly earnings
rose 0.4% m/m, their strongest monthly pace since November 2025.
Manufacturing activity also strengthened, with the ISM rising to 55.5,

and the non-manufacturing index easing to a still buoyant 54.5.
To watch next week: October CPI (Tuesday); October PPI (Friday).
JAPAN
Wage growth accelerates. Scheduled contractual earnings increased
3.7% y/y in September and total wage growth accelerated to 4.6%,
helped by strong bonus payments. Real wage growth remained posi -
tive at 2.2% y/y.
EMERGING ECONOMIES
Portfolio outflows. According to IIF data, non-resident portfolio invest-
ment flows recorded USD33.8bn of outflows in October, the largest

monthly outflow since March and up from USD19.3bn in September.
Equities accounted for USD31.7bn, led by USD28.3bn from emerging
Asia, as doubts over AI-investment returns and profit-taking hit North
Asian markets particularly hard. Taiwan and South Korea suffered a
further sharp market correction in the first week of November.

### Page 2

7
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.41 / 9 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ASIA
PMI: expanding except for the Philippines. The ASEAN PMI rose to 53.1
in October from 52.1, pointing to faster expansion. India remained ro -
bust despite a slight decline (54.7). In Taiwan and South Korea, the
manufacturing PMIs declined but remained expansionary, at 54.9 and
51.3, respectively. Indonesia’s real GDP growth accelerated to 5.5% y/y

in Q3 2026 from 5.3% in Q2 marking its fastest pace since 2013.
China
The PBOC steps in as equity correction deepens. The central bank in -
creased its use of liquidity tools designed to support equity purchases,
including the special relending facility for share buybacks and stake in-
creases and a third round of the Securities, Funds and Insurance Com-
panies Swap Facility, for which 31 institutions applied for more than
RMB100bn. The measures follow heavy stock-market losses between

June and October. The PBOC also raised the reserve-requirement ratio
on foreign-currency deposits to 6% from 4%, effective 15 November, in
an effort to slow the renminbi's appreciation.
Weak domestic inflation pressure: In October, CPI inflation slowed to
0.7% y/y from 0.9% in September. Fuel prices increased by 20.2% y/y,
food prices fell -2.9% and core inflation slowed to 0.8% y/y from 1.0%.
Producer price inflation continued to rise (reaching 5.7% vs. 5.0% in

September), principally driven by higher energy and electronics prices.
To watch next week: External-trade data (Tuesday).
EMEA
Central Europe
PMIs expansionary except in Romania. Poland’s PMI hit the 50-mark
for the first time since April 2025 thanks to export orders. PMI was
also higher in Czech Rep. (55), supported by new orders, output and
employment. Hungary’s PMI fell marginally to 53.1.
Poland and Czech Republic central banks hold and hike respectively,

as expected.
Gulf
PMIs resilient except Qatar (deteriorating further to 46.3).
Saudi Arabia: Massive budget deficit in sight. Saudi Arabia recorded a
budget deficit of USD22.5bn in Q3, narrowing by 5% y/y. But over the
first nine months of 2026, budget deficit reached USD65.1bn, up from
USD48bn in 2025, despite a rather good revenue performance (+2.8%
total; +7.8% for non-oil revenues; -1.3% for oil revenues). The 8% rise in
spending in Q1-Q3 partly due to the war will make it difficult to meet

the 2026 revised target (deficit of 4.9% of GDP).
LATIN AMERICA
PMIs remain weak across the region, with only Colombia in expansion.
Brazil’s central bank pauses rate cuts, whereas Mexico’s central bank
cuts rates whilst signalling an upcoming pause. On 4 November, Bra -
zil’s central bank held its Selic rate at 13.75%, interrupting a series of
five consecutive cuts, amid rising inflation (4.6% y/y in September, up
from 4.2%), a sharp post-election real depreciation on fiscal concerns,

and higher energy prices. The central bank also revised up its 2027
inflation forecast to 4.7% (from 3.9%) and removed forward guidance
hinting at further cuts. Mexico’s central bank unexpectedly cut its po -
licy rate by 25bp to 6.25% on 5 November. This decision came despite
above-target inflation (3.5% against a 3% target), a peso depreciation
of more than 9% against USD since September, and relatively strong Q3
GDP growth (3.2% y/y). The central bank signaled that rates would now

remain on hold for an extended period.
COMMODITIES
Geopolitical relief delivers a dramatic energy-price reversal. Mar -
kets have priced in the reduced risk of short-term conflict escalation
through a sharp decline in prices. Beyond this, however, they lack di -
rection, wavering between positive signals (the resumption of indirect
negotiations between the belligerents) and less favorable ones (in -
creased vessel damage in the strait). Brent dropped 7.6% over the week

to USD96.8/bbl as markets priced a reduced risk of near-term conflict
escalation. European gas prices fell almost in lockstep, declining 7.7%
to EUR87.6/MWh. US commercial crude inventories continued to de -
cline, although more modestly than over the preceding four weeks.
Diesel crack spread’s reaction to US elections has been much more
limited than for crude. EU gas storage ended the refilling season at 75%
of capacity (vs. 80% goal).
y/y = year-on-year

m/m = month-on-month
bp = basis point
pp = percentage point
mb = million barrels
MBtu = Million British Thermal Units

GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Air
Coming Out Of AI Bubble
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 8 Nov 2026

### Page 2

The AI selloff went global last week. In the US, Democrats swept
Congress in the midterm elections on Tuesday, November 3, surfing on a
huge wave of voter hostility toward new data centers. On Thursday,
investors dumped the AI trade worldwide, and Asia’s chipmakers took the
brunt of the wipeout. Overseas earnings are still climbing, but valuations
keep falling. We see the AI backlash as a speed bump, not a roadblock, at
home and abroad. Here’s more:

I. Stock Market Performance
Global sector leadership flipped this week according to the FTSE indexes
(chart). Defensive sectors held up best, with Real Estate up 2.2% and
Consumer Staples up 0.7%, while Consumer Discretionary led with a
2.7% gain. Technology (-6.7%) and Telecommunications (-7.4%) were
near the bottom. Energy was the worst performer, down 10.4%, as fears
of a post-midterm re-escalation in the Middle East faded. The FTSE World
index fell 2.5%.

The US has been gaining ground since we lowered our "Go Global"
investment strategy to market weight on October 18. The US MSCI’s
share of the All Country World MSCI’s market cap has climbed to 65.3%,
up from 62.4% in March (chart). The gains have come at the expense of
everyone else. The Emerging Markets MSCI’s share has dropped to
11.1% from 12.1% in mid-October.

### Page 3

The AI-heavy markets were at the bottom of the country ETF rankings this
week (chart). Taiwan fell 11.7% in dollars, South Korea 10.4%, and Japan
5.0%. This year’s laggards caught bids. South Africa led with an 8.5%
gain, followed by India at 6.6%. The US ETF fell 2.7%, slightly worse than
the All Country World ex-US ETF’s 1.8% decline.
Both the US and overseas ETFs have pulled back from their recent highs
(chart). Both remain in solid uptrends that began in late 2022.

### Page 4

II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
The All Country World ex-US MSCI’s forward earnings rose to yet another
record high this week (chart). It has soared since 2025 after going
nowhere for most of the previous decade.
Forward earnings is up 41.0% y/y, while forward revenues is up 11.2%
(chart). During solid economic expansions, forward revenue growth tends
to hover around 5%. Today’s double-digit pace confirms an economic
boom underpinning the global bull market.

### Page 5

Valuations continue to fall even as earnings soar (chart). The US MSCI’s
forward P/E is down to 18.9. Japan’s has dropped to 14.2 from 14.7 a
week ago. The All Country World ex-US trades at 12.0. The Emerging
Markets MSCI has slipped to single digits and is at 8.6 now.
III. Bonds
Sovereign bonds continued to rally worldwide this week as investors fled
the AI trade (chart). The US 10-year Treasury yield fell to 4.86%. The UK's
yield remains the highest in the group, at 5.16%, followed by Australia’s

5.00%. France’s yield has dropped to 4.77%, back below the US yield.
Japan’s yield is down to 2.63%.

### Page 6

IV. Focus on South Korea & Taiwan
South Korea's forward earnings is still rising to record highs, though more
slowly than earlier in the year (chart). The stock market isn’t buying it. The
MSCI South Korea stock price index is down more than 45% from its
record high. Its forward P/E has collapsed to 3.4, the lowest in over 20
years. Investors are betting that analysts will have to slash their earnings
estimates.
Taiwan tells the same story, but with a more sensible valuation. Its forward

earnings is also at a record high. Its forward P/E has dropped to 14.4 from
23.2 earlier this year (chart).

### Page 7

Fundamental data suggest South Korea and Taiwan should continue to
benefit from US demand for technology hardware. US imports of selected
technologies rose to a record $61.3 billion in September (chart).
Asia supplies much of that hardware. US imports from Vietnam rose to
$28.7 billion in September, and imports from Taiwan rose to $28.4 billion
(chart). Imports from South Korea rose to $19.2 billion. A slower US data
center buildout would delay that demand, not derail it. We would view a

slower pace as more sustainable over a longer period of time.

### Page 8

clause.

## Document 374: Hotter US inflation strengthens the case for a December Fed hike

Source PDF: all report pdf/Hotter US inflation strengthens the case for a December Fed hike.pdf

### Page 1

US consumer price inﬂation came in hotter than
expected, with headline CPI rising 0.4% month-on-month and
core CPI up 0.3%, both a tenth of a percentage point above
expectations. No year-on-year rates were published because
the government shutdown meant no price data was collected
in October 2025
James Knightley
Used vehicle prices fell sharply in October
There was some softness in the details of today’s US CPI report,
with new vehicle prices rising just 0.2% month-on-month and used

vehicle prices falling 1.2%. But other components were hotter:
medical care commodities rose 0.5%, transportation services
jumped 0.8%, while shelter, the largest component of the CPI
basket, increased 0.3%. Overall, headline CPI rose 0.4% and core
0.3%, both 0.1ppt above consensus.
Following on from Fed Governor Chris Waller's comments earlier
today that he backs a 25bp hike in December and could even opt
for a 50bp hike, Treasury yields have pushed higher, with the 10Y

costs and lower energy prices amid weak wage growth dampening
price pressures in the economy.

### Page 4

◆ The economy remains on the recovery track despite weakness in some
areas
Annualized real GDP growth in the second quarter was +1.1% QoQ, with growth
staying in positive territory for three straight quarters. Although personal
consumption exhibited weakness and capital investment declined, overall growth
was lifted by foreign demand. Exports, particularly of services, increased, and the
drop in imports of Middle Eastern crude oil also helped foreign demand turn positive.

◆ Housing starts are weak
Housing starts surged 18.6% YoY in June. They have continued to rise on a YoY
basis, but the jump was mainly due to a rebound from the slump in the same month
of the previous year. The recovery in the number of starts is actually moderate,
reflecting higher interest rates and soaring construction costs.
◆ Earthquake impact will need to be watched
Damage from the earthquake that occurred in Kumamoto Prefecture at the end of

July is weighing on economic activity in the affected areas. Although moves toward
recovery can be seen, with many companies having already resumed operations,
the impact could become prolonged in the following scenarios:
The first is if damage to capital stock such as factories and roads has been severe.
Some companies have sustained damage to production facilities, making an early
resumption of operations difficult in some cases.
The second is if a downturn in tourism demand occurs. According to some media

reports, there has been an uptick in cancellations of accommodation reservations in
Kumamoto Prefecture and neighboring prefectures since the earthquake. After the
2016 Kumamoto earthquake, the decline in travel demand from Japanese visitors
was short lived, but lower demand from foreign tourists persisted for a long period.
Annualized real GDP growth was +1.1% quarter over quarter (QoQ) in Q2 2026
